           This file contains birth records from the Gluckstal 
           Parishes, South Russia for the decade 185x.
           This information was compiled by Dale Wahl and 
           coworkers January 23, 1997 from the St. Petersburg
           Lutheran Evangelical archives published by the LDS
           and edited by Marty McMahon. -re (1-23-1997)
